# Ticket 970: v5.2 replication pass

22 Oct 2026. This follow-up to 970 (merged into `redesign/906` as f44c50dc) copies the v5.2 boards exactly for every
control the card ships, under the owner's rule (Marcello, 22 Oct): "replicate 5.2". It goes into `redesign/906` as a
new PR. The coordinator runs the review, PR and merge. No Trello writes, no merge into `redesign/906`, no force-push.

## Result

| Result | Rows | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| FIXED | 722 | The build differed from the board (or had no such piece) and now equals it |
| MATCH | 404 | Equal before and after |
| MEASUREMENT | 68 | Computed values differ but the pixels do not, for a stated reason (section "Measurement rows") |
| RECORDED | 52 | Boards disagree with each other or with their own text; built to the owner's or coordinator's default |
| Total | 1,246 | Every 970-owned control instance and state drawn on, or derived from, the in-scope boards |

Nothing is left REAL (a fixable difference) or NO-IMPL (a board piece with no code).

## Method

1. Boards in scope: G0 tokens, G1 rules, G2 to G11, G12a, G14 section 5, G17. The board sources
   (`v5.2-src/shared/project/*.dc.html` with `qt.css`, `g31.css`, `glass.css`, `glass-v3.1.css`) were rendered in
   headless Chrome for Testing (temporary profile, deleted) at each board's native size, with Poppins and Figtree
   loaded. `project/qt.css` is byte-identical to `qt-v5.css` v5.2 (sha256 3b761a9b...), and `glass-v3.1.css` to v3.2.1
   (f9d992f4...). The PDFs for G0, G1, G12a, G14 and G17 (and G4, G8, G9) match the source renders apart from
   anti-aliasing (`evidence/replicate/board/pdf-vs-src.md`). G2, G14 and G17 draw no 970 control.
2. Reference: 107 rows drawn on the boards, plus 1,139 rows derived from probe pages that put each board class in
   every state and context (paper, navy, glass content zone, glass band, plate, opaque commit step) with
   `CSS.forcePseudoState`. Each row holds getComputedStyle values for the element, `::before`, `::after` and its parts,
   with token names (`evidence/replicate/board/inventory.json`, `rows.md`, `crops/`).
3. Build: a throwaway customer-surface harness route rendered every component, variant, size, state and context.
   It ran on a local production build (`bun run build`, `PORT=3000 bun run start`) at 390 (DPR 2), 768 (DPR 2) and
   1440 (DPR 1), with states from CDP forced states, which matched real Tab, hover and press. The three sample pages
   (/my-offers, /notifications, /offer/{id}) were captured in loading, error, empty and data states with local
   fixtures (a seeded fixture user, every /api/v1 and socket request answered locally, no sign-in, no external host).
   The harness was deleted before both commits and a clean build has no harness route.
4. Compare: `tools/replicate/compare.py` matched every board row to its code row: colours exact, lengths to 0.5 px,
   width only where the board sets it. `after.py` records before and after. Side-by-side crops are in
   `evidence/replicate/side/` (`*__after.png` for the final build, `*__pdf.png` for the C12 and E1d loading buttons
   against their PDFs).
5. Owner rules held: customer-scoped classes only, default-surface class strings unchanged, tokens only (no hex in
   components), no code comments, bun only, no stash, reset or clean, no node_modules symlink.

Evidence root: `/Users/starbird/Workspace/Code/qic/.scratch/970/evidence/replicate/`. Scripts:
`/Users/starbird/Workspace/Code/qic/.scratch/970/tools/replicate/`.

## Measurement rows

| Rows | Component and property | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| 47 | Amount figure height | The board `.amt` box holds the unit on the same line, which grows the line box; the code measures the figure span alone. Same baseline and glyphs |
| 45 | Amount unit line height, height, numeric features | The board nests the unit inside `.amt` and it inherits the figure's line height and features; the unit has no digits. No visible effect |
| 12 | FieldError height | The board text wraps to more lines in its container |
| 12 | Skeleton height | Board sets each skeleton's size inline |
| 10 | Field label gap | Gap on a label with one text child; no effect |
| 8 | Checkbox, radio, input outline in a glass band | The harness had no such control in a band, so the glass content zone was compared; the band rule (`.gl-band :focus-visible`, `--foreground`, -2 px) applies to any control in a band |
| 3 | Chip and checkbox-label height | Line box content (icon or unit) on the drawn line |

68 board rows in all. Cursor is not compared on the 108 loading-button rows: the boards have no loading class (the
probe composes `.btn` and `.spinner`, so its pointer is the plain button's), and the code disables a loading button
(`not-allowed`). Those rows are judged on every other property.

## Review rounds

1. First independent review (opus55-high, 1ad8734d): NEEDS_FIXES with six findings. It confirmed the replication values
   (25+ rows re-measured in Chrome), the default surface and the money rules. Fixed in 9378896f and 950c0d89:
   1. HIGH: the glass-band field outline also hit 968's Jump palette input, giving two rings where G9 draws one. It is
      now scoped to Input, the Select trigger, textarea and AmountInput (`globals.css:834-835`). A real render of the
      focused Jump palette at 1440 and 390 shows one ring, on the label
      (`evidence/replicate/fix/jump/jump-focus.json` and PNGs).
   2. MEDIUM: the open-trigger look applied to every customer Button with `aria-expanded`, on paper too. It now
      applies only to `.gl-trigger` Buttons and icon Buttons in the `.gcap.m3` capsule, as glass v3.2.1 draws it
      (`globals.css:845`; no `customer:aria-expanded` left in `button.tsx`).
   3. LOW: a summary-size Amount on navy now turns its unit white as well (`globals.css:841`, `data-size`).
   4. LOW: the reduced-motion "Loading..." stand-in is hidden only inside a loading Button. A standalone Spinner
      keeps it beside the still ring, as 970's AC4.1 requires (`globals.css:765`, DESIGN.md section 6).
   5. LOW: five evidence crops re-cut from element boxes after scrolling into view, and checked by eye.
   6. LOW: `after.json` no longer compares cursor on the 108 loading-button rows (the boards draw no loading
      cursor). button-463 is compared with its real equivalent and is FIXED.
2. Re-review (opus55-high, 0fb01120): items 1 to 5 fixed. Item 6 still had cursor entries marked MATCH, so I removed
   them (216 entries). It also noted `data-size` indentation and a needless test cast, both fixed in 950c0d89. One
   latent note remains, with no current caller affected: the CSS hooks find a Button by `data-testid="qt-button"`, and
   a caller's own `data-testid` replaces it.
